{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A9dcjCyjq7pPQvYUwXVjnS","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nPlan 015: Wild-Data Calibration — WikiText-103 evaluation.\\\\n\\\\nEvaluates the trained RF depth estimator on non-controlled human text (WikiText-103).\\\\nCompares predicted depth distribution against controlled d0/d1/d2/d3 synthetic data.\\\\n\\\\nOutput: results/plan_015_wild_data/\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport sys\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nfrom collections import Counter\\\\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\\\\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\\\\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\\\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, confusion_matrix, classification_report\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\\\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\\\n\\\\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\\\\n    \\\\\\\"mean_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"skewness_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"p10_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p25_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p50_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p75_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"mean_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"entropy_of_surprisal\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"type_token_ratio\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"hapax_ratio\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"bigram_entropy\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"trigram_entropy\\\\\\\",\\\\n    \\\\\\\"rep_2gram\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"rep_3gram\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"rep_4gram\\\\\\\",\\\\n]\\\\n\\\\nPROJECT_DIR = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\\\\\\\"\\\\nMODEL_PATH = \\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\\\\\\\"\\\\nFEATURES_PATH = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \\\\\\\"data/features_all.jsonl\\\\\\\")\\\\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \\\\\\\"results/plan_015_wild_data\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\\\\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\\\\n    total = len(ngrams)\\\\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\\\\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\\\\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\\\\n    if n_tokens == 0:\\\\n        return {\\\\n            \\\\\\\"type_token_ratio\\\\\\\": 0.0, \\\\\\\"hapax_ratio\\\\\\\": 0.0,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"bigram_entropy\\\\\\\": 0.0, \\\\\\\"trigram_entropy\\\\\\\": 0.0,\\\\n            \\\\\\\"rep_2gram\\\\\\\": 0.0, \\\\\\\"rep_3gram\\\\\\\": 0.0, \\\\\\\"rep_4gram\\\\\\\": 0.0,\\\\n        }\\\\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\\\\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\\\\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\\\\n\\\\n    def ngram_entropy(ids, n):\\\\n        if len(ids) < n:\\\\n            return 0.0\\\\n        ngrams = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\\\\n        c = Counter(ngrams)\\\\n        total = sum(c.values())\\\\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\\\\n        return float(entropy(probs))\\\\n\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"type_token_ratio\\\\\\\": ttr,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"hapax_ratio\\\\\\\": hapax,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"bigram_entropy\\\\\\\": ngram_entropy(token_ids, 2),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"trigram_entropy\\\\\\\": ngram_entropy(token_ids, 3),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"rep_2gram\\\\\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"rep_3gram\\\\\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"rep_4gram\\\\\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\\\\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\\\\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mean_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"skewness_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"p10_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p25_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p50_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p75_ppl\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\",\\\\n            \\\\\\\"mean_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"entropy_of_surprisal\\\\\\\"]}\\\\n\\\\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\\\\n    result = {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"mean_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"var_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.var(ppl)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"skewness_ppl\\\\\\\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\\\\\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p10_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p25_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p50_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p75_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\\\\n        \\\\\\\"p90_ppl\\\\\\\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\\\\n    }\\\\n\\\\n    surprisal = losses_np\\\\n    result[\\\\\\\"mean_surprisal\\\\\\\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\\\\n    result[\\\\\\\"var_surprisal\\\\\\\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\\\\n\\\\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n# Plan 015 extension: C4 web-crawl calibration supplement.\\n# Tests RF depth estimator on diverse web text (C4 validation) to complement WikiText-103.\\n# Uses Pythia-1.4B for feature extraction (same as plan_015) to match RF training features.\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy, ks_2samp\\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\\nfrom sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler\\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\\n    \\\"mean_ppl\\\", \\\"var_ppl\\\", \\\"skewness_ppl\\\", \\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\",\\n    \\\"p10_ppl\\\", \\\"p25_ppl\\\", \\\"p50_ppl\\\", \\\"p75_ppl\\\", \\\"p90_ppl\\\",\\n    \\\"mean_surprisal\\\", \\\"var_surprisal\\\", \\\"entropy_of_surprisal\\\",\\n    \\\"type_token_ratio\\\", \\\"hapax_ratio\\\", \\\"bigram_entropy\\\", \\\"trigram_entropy\\\",\\n    \\\"rep_2gram\\\", \\\"rep_3gram\\\", \\\"rep_4gram\\\",\\n]\\n\\nPROJECT_DIR = \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\\\"\\nMODEL_PATH = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \\\"../models/pythia-1.4b\\\")\\nFEATURES_PATH = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \\\"data/features_all.jsonl\\\")\\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \\\"results/plan_015_wild_data\\\")\\nWIKI_RESULTS_PATH = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \\\"results.json\\\")\\n\\n\\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\\n        return 0.0\\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\\n    total = len(ngrams)\\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\n\\n\\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\\n    if n_tokens == 0:\\n        return {\\n            \\\"type_token_ratio\\\": 0.0, \\\"hapax_ratio\\\": 0.0,\\n            \\\"bigram_entropy\\\": 0.0, \\\"trigram_entropy\\\": 0.0,\\n            \\\"rep_2gram\\\": 0.0, \\\"rep_3gram\\\": 0.0, \\\"rep_4gram\\\": 0.0,\\n        }\\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\\n\\n    def ngram_entropy(ids, n):\\n        if len(ids) < n:\\n            return 0.0\\n        ngrams = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\\n        c = Counter(ngrams)\\n        total = sum(c.values())\\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\\n        return float(entropy(probs))\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"type_token_ratio\\\": ttr,\\n        \\\"hapax_ratio\\\": hapax,\\n        \\\"bigram_entropy\\\": ngram_entropy(token_ids, 2),\\n        \\\"trigram_entropy\\\": ngram_entropy(token_ids, 3),\\n        \\\"rep_2gram\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\\n        \\\"rep_3gram\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\\n        \\\"rep_4gram\\\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\\n            \\\"mean_ppl\\\", \\\"var_ppl\\\", \\\"skewness_ppl\\\", \\\"kurtosis_ppl\\\",\\n            \\\"p10_ppl\\\", \\\"p25_ppl\\\", \\\"p50_ppl\\\", \\\"p75_ppl\\\", \\\"p90_ppl\\\",\\n            \\\"mean_surprisal\\\", \\\"var_surprisal\\\", \\\"entropy_of_surprisal\\\"]}\\n\\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\\n    result = {\\n        \\\"mean_ppl\\\": len(surprisal) // 5))\\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\\n        probs\\n\\n... \\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nExtract statistical features from depth-level JSONL files using frozen Pythia-1.4B.\\\\n\\\\nInput:  data/depth_{0,1,2,3}.jsonl — each line: {\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\": \\\\\\\"...\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"depth\\\\\\\": N, ...}\\\\nOutput: data/features.jsonl — each line: {\\\\\\\"depth\\\\\\\": N, \\\\\\\"sample_id\\\\\\\": M, \\\\\\\"mean_ppl\\\\\\\": ..., ...}\\\\n\\\\nDependencies: torch, transformers, scipy, numpy, tqdm\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport argparse\\\\nimport json\\\\nimport os\\\\nimport numpy as np\\\\nimport torch\\\\nfrom collections import Counter\\\\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\\\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef parse_args():\\\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\\\\\"Extract statistical features from depth JSONL files.\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--data_dir\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"data/\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--model_path\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--output_path\\\\\\\", default=\\\\\\\"data/features.jsonl\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--gpu\\\\\\\", type=int, default=1)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--batch_size\\\\\\\", type=int, default=16)\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_samples\\\\\\\", type=int, default=0, help=\\\\\\\"Max samples per depth (0=all)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    p.add_argument(\\\\\\\"--max_depth\\\\\\\", type=int, default=3, help=\\\\\\\"Maximum depth index to load (default: 3 for depth_0..depth_3)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return p.parse_args()\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef load_depth_data(data_dir, max_samples, max_depth=3):\\\\n    depth_data = {}\\\\n    for d in range(max_depth + 1):\\\\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\\\\\\\"depth_{d}.jsonl\\\\\\\")\\\\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\\\\n            print(f\\\\\\\"[WARN] {fpath} not found, skipping\\\\\\\")\\\\n            continue\\\\n        records = []\\\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\\\n            for line in f:\\\\n                line = line.strip()\\\\n                if not line:\\\\n                    continue\\\\n                try:\\\\n                    records.append(json.loads(line))\\\\n                except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\\\n                    print(f\\\\\\\"[WARN] Bad JSON in {fpath}: {e}\\\\\\\")\\\\n        if max_samples > 0 and len(records) > max_samples:\\\\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\\\n            idx = rng.choice(len(records), max_samples, replace=False)\\\\n            records = [records[i] for i in sorted(idx)]\\\\n        depth_data[d] = records\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"Loaded depth {d}: {len(records)} samples\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return depth_data\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_truncation_length(depth_data, tokenizer):\\\\n    depth_medians = {}\\\\n    for d, records in sorted(depth_data.items()):\\\\n        lengths = []\\\\n        for r in records:\\\\n            ids = tokenizer.encode(r[\\\\\\\"text\\\\\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\\\n            lengths.append(len(ids))\\\\n        med = int(np.median(lengths))\\\\n        depth_medians[d] = med\\\\n        print(f\\\\\\\"  Depth {d}: n={len(lengths)}, median={med}, min={min(lengths)}, max={max(lengths)}, mean={np.mean(lengths):.0f}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    min_median = min(depth_medians.values())\\\\n    print(f\\\\\\\"  => Truncation length (min median): {min_median} tokens\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return min_median\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\\\\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\\\\n        return 0.0\\\\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\\\\n    total = len(ngrams)\\\\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\\\\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\\\n\\\", \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nExtract statistical features from depth-level JSONL files using frozen Pythia-1.4B.\\n\\nInput:  data/depth_{0,1,2,3}.jsonl — each line: {\\\"text\\\": numpy, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Extract statistical features from depth JSONL files.\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--data_dir\\\", default=\\\"data/\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_path\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", default=\\\"data/features.jsonl\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=1)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_samples\\\", type=int, default=0, help=\\\"Max samples per depth (0=all)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_depth\\\", type=int, default=3, help=\\\"Maximum depth index to load (default: 3 for depth_0..depth_3)\\\")\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef load_depth_data(data_dir, max_samples, max_depth=3):\\n    depth_data = {}\\n    for d in range(max_depth + 1):\\n        fpath = os.path.join(data_dir, f\\\"depth_{d}.jsonl\\\")\\n        if not os.path.exists(fpath):\\n            print(f\\\"[WARN] {fpath} not found, skipping\\\")\\n            continue\\n        records = []\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\n            for line in f:\\n                line = line.strip()\\n                if not line:\\n                    continue\\n                try:\\n                    records.append(json.loads(line))\\n                except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\n                    print(f\\\"[WARN] Bad JSON in {fpath}: {e}\\\")\\n        if max_samples > 0 and len(records) > max_samples:\\n            rng = np.random.RandomState(42)\\n            idx = rng.choice(len(records), max_samples, replace=False)\\n            records = [records[i] for i in sorted(idx)]\\n        depth_data[d] = records\\n        print(f\\\"Loaded depth {d}: {len(records)} samples\\\")\\n    return depth_data\\n\\n\\ndef compute_truncation_length(depth_data, tokenizer):\\n    depth_medians = {}\\n    for d, records in sorted(depth_data.items()):\\n        lengths = []\\n        for r in records:\\n            ids = tokenizer.encode(r[\\\"text\\\"], add_special_tokens=False)\\n            lengths.append(len(ids))\\n        med = int(np.median(lengths))\\n        depth_medians[d] = med\\n        print(f\\\"  Depth {d}: n={len(lengths)}, median={med}, min={min(lengths)}, max={max(lengths)}, mean={np.mean(lengths):.0f}\\\")\\n    min_median = min(depth_medians.values())\\n    print(f\\\"  => Truncation length (min median): {min_median} tokens\\\")\\n    return min_median\\n\\n\\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\\n        return 0.0\\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\\n    total = len(ngrams)\\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": `load_dataset()` with `streaming=True` parameter, specifying snapshots, languages, and the configuration name. Here's a basic example:\n\n```python\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\n\nds_iterator = load_dataset(\n    \"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\",\n    snapshots=[\"2023-14\"],\n    languages=[\"en\"],\n    name=\"default\",\n    streaming=True\n)\n```\n\nYou can also load a sample configuration in streaming mode using the `sample-10B` configuration with `trust_remote_code=True`.\n\n## About Streaming\n\nLoading a dataset in streaming mode creates an IterableDataset instance, and the data is downloaded progressively as you iterate over the dataset. An IterableDataset is useful for iterative jobs like training a model, though you shouldn't use it for jobs that require random access to examples.\n\n## Dataset Information\n\nRedPajama-V2 is an open dataset for training large language models that includes over 100B text documents coming from 84 CommonCrawl snapshots and processed using the CCNet pipeline.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the `load_dataset()` with `streaming=True` parameter, specifying snapshots, languages, and the configuration name. Here's a basic example:\n\n```python\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\n\nds_iterator = load_dataset(\n    \"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\",\n    snapshots=[\"2023-14\"],\n    languages=[\"en\"],\n    name=\"default\",\n    streaming=True\n)\n```\n\nYou can also load a sample configuration in streaming mode using the `sample-10B` configuration with `trust_remote_code=True`.\n\n## About Streaming\n\nLoading a dataset in streaming mode creates an IterableDataset instance, and the data is downloaded progressively as you iterate over the dataset. An IterableDataset is useful for iterative jobs like training a model, though you shouldn't use it for jobs that require random access to examples.\n\n## Dataset Information\n\nRedPajama-V2 is an open dataset for training large language models that includes over 100B text documents coming from 84 CommonCrawl snapshots and processed using the CCNet pipeline."],"durationSeconds":5.589775475000002}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01FmHDRks9REFvL5LVq2YGh8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0111H5PehyxtaSGLPb54BRSe","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/artifacts/plan_015_wild_data_v2.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & Dolma.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0), Dolma (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"redpajama\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"sample-10B\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True,\n                 extra=dict(snapshots=[\"2023-14\"], languages=[\"en\"])),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"dolma\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma3_pool\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True, trust_remote_code=cfg.get(\"trust_remote_code\", False))\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            if \"extra\" in cfg:\n                kwargs.update(cfg[\"extra\"])\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"redpajama\", \"dolma\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"redpajama\": 0, \"dolma\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} {'-'*20} {'-'*6} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\")\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} {clf_name:<20s} {r['n_samples']:>6d} \"\n                  f\"{r['d0_accuracy']:>8.2f} {r['fpr']:>8.2f} \"\n                  f\"{r['mean_prob']['d0']:>8.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Per-depth breakdown:\")\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            pcts = r[\"pred_pct\"]\n            print(f\"    {ds_name}/{clf_name}: \"\n                  f\"d0={pcts['d0']:.2f}% python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & Dolma.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0), Dolma (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"redpajama\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"sample-10B\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True,\n                 extra=dict(snapshots=[\"2023-14\"], languages=[\"en\"])),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"dolma\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma3_pool\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True, trust_remote_code=cfg.get(\"trust_remote_code\", False))\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            if \"extra\" in cfg:\n                kwargs.update(cfg[\"extra\"])\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0) & Dolma (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"redpajama\", \"dolma\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"redpajama\": 0, \"dolma\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} {'-'*20} {'-'*6} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\")\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} {clf_name:<20s} {r['n_samples']:>6d} \"\n                  f\"{r['d0_accuracy']:>8.2f} {r['fpr']:>8.2f} \"\n                  f\"{r['mean_prob']['d0']:>8.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Per-depth breakdown:\")\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            pcts = r[\"pred_pct\"]\n            print(f\"    {ds_name}/{clf_name}: \"\n                  f\"d0={pcts['d0']:.2f}% d1={pcts['d1']:.2f}% \"\n                  f\"d2={pcts['d2']:.2f}% d3={pcts['d3']:.2f}%\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Total time: {elapsed_total:.0f}s\")\n    print(f\"  Results: {OUTPUT_DIR}/\")\n {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QnENLomD8mXtFWucjVn7er","type":"tool_result","content":"Web search results for query: \"HuggingFace fineweb dataset load_dataset streaming python 2026\"\n\nLinks: [{\"title\":\"Stream · Hugging Face\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/stream\"},{\"title\":\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb-2 · how to load the entire dataset\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuggingFaceFW/fineweb-2/discussions/8\"},{\"title\":\"Stream\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v3.6.0/en/stream\"},{\"title\":\"Speeding up Streaming of Large Datasets (FineWeb)? - 🤗Datasets - Hugging Face Forums\",\"url\":\"https://discuss.huggingface.co/t/speeding-up-streaming-of-large-datasets-fineweb/90714\"},{\"title\":\"Load a Dataset in Streaming mode — datasets 1.10.1 documentation\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.10.1/dataset_streaming.html\"},{\"title\":\"More easily support streaming local files · Issue #7084 · huggingface/datasets\",\"url\":\"https://github.com/huggingface/datasets/issues/7084\"},{\"title\":\"Loading a Dataset — datasets 1.11.0 documentation\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.11.0/loading_datasets.html\"},{\"title\":\"Load · Hugging Face\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/loading\"},{\"title\":\"Loading methods · Hugging Face\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/package_reference/loading_methods\"},{\"title\":\"Load a Dataset in Streaming mode — datasets 1.11.0 documentation\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.11.0/dataset_streaming.html\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's information about using HuggingFace's `load_dataset` with streaming for the FineWeb dataset:\n\n## Basic Usage\n\nTo stream the FineWeb dataset, you can use `load_dataset()` with `streaming=True`:\n\n```python\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\n\ndataset = load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True)\nprint(next(iter(dataset)))\n```\n\n## Key Benefits\n\nThe English split of the HuggingFaceFW/fineweb dataset is 45 terabytes, but you can use it instantly with streaming. Loading a dataset in streaming mode creates a new dataset type instance known as an IterableDataset.\n\n## Advanced Options\n\nYou can customize streaming with additional parameters:\n\n**Select specific columns:**\nYou can specify columns using the `columns` parameter: `load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True, columns=[\"url\", \"date\"])`\n\n**Apply filters:**\nYou can filter data using the `filters` parameter, such as filtering by language_score: `load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True, filters=[(\"language_score\", \">=\", 0.99)])`\n\n**Shuffling:**\nYou can shuffle a streaming dataset with `dataset.shuffle(seed=42, buffer_size=10_000)`\n\n## How Streaming Works\n\nWhen a dataset is in streaming mode, you can iterate over it directly without having to download the entire dataset, and the data are downloaded progressively as you iterate over the dataset.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a2ce5c46-8d87-471b-bc3b-e8b0277f0ee9","uuid":"b2dbf450-2752-46ba-b3d7-dbc90da54ec9","timestamp":"2026-05-16T00:41:15.302Z","tool_use_result":{"query":"HuggingFace fineweb dataset load_dataset streaming python 2026","results":[{"tool_use_id":"srvtoolu_01RVyKA9hS8hp3eD3GEkdkNc","content":[{"title":"Stream · Hugging Face","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/stream"},{"title":"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb-2 · how to load the entire dataset","url":"https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuggingFaceFW/fineweb-2/discussions/8"},{"title":"Stream","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v3.6.0/en/stream"},{"title":"Speeding up Streaming of Large Datasets (FineWeb)? - 🤗Datasets - Hugging Face Forums","url":"https://discuss.huggingface.co/t/speeding-up-streaming-of-large-datasets-fineweb/90714"},{"title":"Load a Dataset in Streaming mode — datasets 1.10.1 documentation","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.10.1/dataset_streaming.html"},{"title":"More easily support streaming local files · Issue #7084 · huggingface/datasets","url":"https://github.com/huggingface/datasets/issues/7084"},{"title":"Loading a Dataset — datasets 1.11.0 documentation","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.11.0/loading_datasets.html"},{"title":"Load · Hugging Face","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/loading"},{"title":"Loading methods · Hugging Face","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/en/package_reference/loading_methods"},{"title":"Load a Dataset in Streaming mode — datasets 1.11.0 documentation","url":"https://huggingface.co/docs/datasets/v1.11.0/dataset_streaming.html"}]},"Based on the search results, here's information about using HuggingFace's `load_dataset` with streaming for the FineWeb dataset:\n\n## Basic Usage\n\nTo stream the FineWeb dataset, you can use `load_dataset()` with `streaming=True`:\n\n```python\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\n\ndataset = load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True)\nprint(next(iter(dataset)))\n```\n\n## Key Benefits\n\nThe English split of the HuggingFaceFW/fineweb dataset is 45 terabytes, but you can use it instantly with streaming. Loading a dataset in streaming mode creates a new dataset type instance known as an IterableDataset.\n\n## Advanced Options\n\nYou can customize streaming with additional parameters:\n\n**Select specific columns:**\nYou can specify columns using the `columns` parameter: `load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True, columns=[\"url\", \"date\"])`\n\n**Apply filters:**\nYou can filter data using the `filters` parameter, such as filtering by language_score: `load_dataset('HuggingFaceFW/fineweb', split='train', streaming=True, filters=[(\"language_score\", \">=\", 0.99)])`\n\n**Shuffling:**\nYou can shuffle a streaming dataset with `dataset.shuffle(seed=42, buffer_size=10_000)`\n\n## How Streaming Works\n\nWhen a dataset is in streaming mode, you can iterate over it directly without having to download the entire dataset, and the data are downloaded progressively as you iterate over the dataset."],"durationSeconds":6.0809020820001605}}

load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n130\t    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n131\t    from datasets import load_dataset\n132\t\n133\t    texts = []\n134\t    skipped = 0\n135\t\n136\t    if dataset_name == \"redpajama\":\n137\t        configs = [\n138\t            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"sample-10B\",\n139\t                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True),\n140\t            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"default\",\n141\t                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True,\n142\t                 extra=dict(snapshots=[\"2023-14\"], languages=[\"en\"])),\n143\t        ]\n144\t    elif dataset_name == \"dolma\":\n145\t        configs = [\n146\t            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma\", split=\"train\",\n147\t                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n148\t            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma3_pool\", split=\"train\",\n149\t                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n150\t        ]\n151\t    else:\n152\t        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n153\t\n154\t    ds = None\n155\t    text_field = \"text\"\n156\t\n157\t    for cfg in configs:\n158\t        try:\n159\t            kwargs = dict(streaming=True, trust_remote_code=cfg.get(\"trust_remote_code\", False))\n160\t            if \"name\" in cfg:\n161\t loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on RedPajama-v2 & Dolma.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: RedPajama-v2 (cuda:0), Dolma (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"redpajama\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"sample-10B\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"togethercomputer/RedPajama-Data-V2\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"raw_content\", trust_remote_code=True,\n                 extra=dict(snapshots=[\"2023-14\"], languages=[\"en\"])),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"dolma\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/dolma3_pool\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\", trust_remote_code=True),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True, trust_remote_code=cfg.get(\"trust_remote_code\", False))\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            if \"extra\" in cfg:\n                kwargs.update(cfg[\"extra\"])\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"raw_content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n
